### Reproduce the prepared service

Install the repository requirements and generate the fixture with
`python examples/parts/holed_plate_v1.py`. Supply a CUDA 12.9 toolkit and OptiX
9.0 headers. Select the architecture explicitly (measured: `sm_120` on RTX 5070,
`sm_89` on L4). Paths below are caller-provided, and each output directory must
be new or empty:

```sh
make build-prepared-service PYTHON=python3 BUILD_DIR="$BUILD_DIR" \
  CUDA_ROOT="$CUDA_ROOT" OPTIX_INCLUDE="$OPTIX_INCLUDE" CUDA_ARCH=sm_120
make verify-prepared-cpu PYTHON=python3 EVIDENCE_DIR="$CPU_EVIDENCE_DIR"
make verify-prepared-gpu PYTHON=python3 BUILD_DIR="$BUILD_DIR" \
  PLATE_STL=examples/parts/holed_plate_v1.stl EVIDENCE_DIR="$GPU_EVIDENCE_DIR"
python scripts/prepared_verifier_controls.py
```

GPU verification requires the caller's exclusive GPU coordination. An optional
`VERIFY_ARGS="--before-probe /path/to/executable-guard"` runs that guard before
each GPU probe. The build and CPU verification do not open GPU contexts.
The verifier writes fresh JSON, complete captures and logs in the requested
evidence directory; committed measurements are never overwritten. A failing
latency gate exits nonzero even when numerical arrays are exact. To construct
the owned service directly, use `mesh_field_owned_service_v1.MeshFieldService`
with the prepared fused library and prepared PTX from `manifest.json`, the
frozen winding library, EDT-large and column-mask libraries; then call
`prepare(vertices, faces, pitch, origin)` and `query()` inside its context manager.

Fresh public-build validation passes: four CPU probes in each of two isolated
runs, comparing 43,445,792 array bytes per run; three GPU probes compare all
85,558,530 bytes and pass all unchanged gates. The public-build RTX warm L calls
span 1.247556–1.472621 ms. Thirteen verifier controls repeat exactly, including
false/missing gates, wrong decisive values, signed-zero/dtype changes, stale
source hashes and corrupted artifacts. The bounded GPU window lasts 19.20 s,
with journal guards before each probe and after completion; lock released.
Evidence: `reports/prepared_public_{build,cpu_first,cpu,gpu,window}.json`,
`reports/field_prepared_service_public_local.json`, and
`reports/prepared_verifier_controls.json`. Full public-build arrays were compared
to the committed captures; duplicate archives remain in the caller's evidence
directory. `reports/prepared_source_cleanup.json` records the sole post-measurement
native source change: removal of trailing whitespace, retaining original hashes.


Build-byte repeat check is retained separately as OWN-GATE-FAIL: four of eight
artifacts differ between two build directories, although both PTX files and both
host-built RT libraries match. The differing CUDA shared objects contain distinct
compiler-generated `tmpxft` source/symbol names; this observation does not prove
those names explain every byte difference. Numerical reproduction is verified;
bit-identical build output is not claimed. See `reports/prepared_build_repeat.json`.

### Whole-repository coverage preregistration

`make verify` must run the two declared CPU groups with unchanged numerical,
source and complete-array gates, then fail full coverage while any current
VERIFIED-FRESH row is unmapped or requires deferred GPU execution. The fourteen
explicit probe rows in the CSG/prepared contracts are the initial mapping;
imported/native libraries do not acquire mapping from imports. Parse the current
status tables, reject duplicate/missing sources and keep per-row source hashes.
`make verify-declared` may pass only that CPU slice and must still report the full
coverage failure. `make verify-inventory` audits mapping without executing tests.
Evidence directories are fresh and GPU execution remains explicitly separate.


### Initial declared-row verification result

`make verify` runs all eleven declared CPU probes successfully, then exits 2 for
incomplete coverage. The initial snapshot has 14 mapped of 158 fresh rows, with
144 unmapped and three mapped GPU probes explicitly deferred. After adding the
verifier/dependency rows, `make verify-declared` repeats the eleven CPU probes
successfully and exits 0 for that selected slice; its full status remains
OWN-GATE-FAIL with 14/161 mapped, 147 unmapped and three GPU-deferred rows.
Both runs compare 83,624,352 complete array bytes, require all original live
gates and check the documented decisive values. Sixteen verifier controls pass.
The nested aggregate additionally requires the exact expected gate set and all
expected probe executions; an empty or invented green receipt cannot pass.

Use `make verify-declared PYTHON=python3` for the current CPU slice,
`make verify PYTHON=python3` for the full-coverage verdict, or
`make verify-inventory PYTHON=python3` to audit mapping without numerical work.
Fresh evidence goes into a unique ignored `.verify-runs/` directory, overridable
with `VERIFY_ARGS="--output /path/to/new-evidence"`. No GPU is initialized by
these commands. Reports: `reports/field_verify_full_initial.json`,
`reports/field_verify_declared.json`, and `reports/verify_controls.json`.

### Recipe selftest verification result

Two independently copied CPU trees reproduce all four complete canonical reports
exactly. All 29 own checks pass: schema 9, coarse thread table 4, rib 4 and selector
12. The public `make verify-recipe PYTHON=python3` route also passes 5/5 aggregate
gates, requiring exact case identities, original per-case/overall gates, complete
report hashes, declared decisive values and pinned recipe sources. Full raw
reports stay in the caller evidence directory; committed receipts contain hashes
and check counts. No recipe implementation or tolerance was changed.

Decisive values include all 21 declared operation types accepted, seven coarse
thread sizes, rib added volumes 720 / 432 mm3, and zero-hit/over-hit selector
controls 0 / 6. Twenty-six verifier controls include trailing non-JSON output,
missing overall gate, omitted/false cases and duplicated case identities.
The current coverage is 22 mapped of 163 fresh rows, 141 unmapped and three GPU
rows deferred. Full coverage remains incomplete. Evidence:
`reports/recipe_selftests_capture.json` and `reports/recipe_verify.json`.


### Adaptive cross-host input audit preregistration

The corrected Modal CPU upload passes all eleven CSG/service probes, but adaptive full-report/array reproduction fails despite all nine own gates and decisive budgets passing. Only three query values per leg differ, in both candidate and SciPy reference, by at most 0.12856914; full dense grids and interface outputs match. Preserve the complete negative captures. Before attributing arithmetic, capture actual generated mesh vertices/faces and every interpolation input in a separate observer around the unchanged probe on both hosts. Require input identity before any same-input numerical claim.

### Canonical adaptive fixture preregistration

The input audit reproduces each host exactly and a mesh-only transplant reproduces the complete remote report/archive locally. Add a separate adaptive probe that sorts the existing oriented face records lexicographically by vertex indices before the frozen raster/query pipeline. Do not reorder vertices, reverse winding, alter triangle contents or change the adaptive algorithm. Keep the original probe and its failed cross-host capture unchanged. Require all nine original own gates, exact original dense grids, input immutability, face-multiset preservation and identical sorting under explicit permutations. Capture ordered faces/vertices/query inputs as well as every complete output; require two exact local runs and full cross-host report/array reproduction with no tolerance changes.

### Adaptive input audit result

The diagnostic passes 7/7 in two identical runs. Both hosts have exactly the
same vertices and oriented triangle multiset; 28 face rows per leg are permuted.
The unchanged seeded index sampler therefore changes 23 of 4000 surface points;
all 8193 interior points match. Reconstructing points from each captured mesh
reproduces every input exactly. Both hosts' complete query/grid/interface outputs
replay on the local CPU, 18,322,720 bytes across eight replays. A mesh-only
transplant into the unchanged local probe reproduces the entire remote report
and all 4,580,680 output bytes. This identifies the first captured difference;
the specific exporter/library instruction causing face order is not identified.

The original Modal verification remains OWN-GATE-FAIL: eleven CSG/service probes
pass; the original adaptive own gates and budgets pass but its full capture
comparison fails on three query values per leg. Full negative outputs, actual
local/remote inputs and the transplant are retained under `reports/adaptive_*`;
`reports/field_verify_modal.json` and `reports/field_verify_modal_run.json` retain
the 112.08 s full attempt. The public CPU audit is
`python probes/field_engine/field_adaptive_input_audit.py` with the normal source path.
One private observer deployment failed before execution because its helper module
was not included; it was stopped and rerun with the same image definition inline.
No GPU resource or local GPU lock was used.


### Selected geometry verification preregistration

The new input audit and canonical fixture receive explicit geometry recipes.
Allow an explicit module selection while recording all omitted module names;
default verification still requires every declared geometry row, including the
original cross-host negative. Each distinct command gets its own fresh source,
fixture and reference-resource tree so one probe cannot overwrite another's
reference archive. The chain and its explicitly executed loft child share only
their one command result. Validate resource hashes before copying and retain all
fresh reports/captures. This selection must not promote full coverage.


### Canonical adaptive cross-host result

The separate canonical fixture passes 14/14 in two exact local runs and on Modal.
All 5,531,960 bytes of ordered vertices/faces/query inputs and complete outputs
match across hosts, as does the complete report. Original dense grids, adaptive
nodes/payload, storage budgets and all original controls remain unchanged.
The new stable sample order has median surface distance 0.13872672617435455;
the original local/cloud medians and their failed report comparison remain intact.

The same Modal job also reproduces both complete optimizer/loft field and recipe
legs, including every byte of the four archived artifacts per leg. All four recipe
selftests pass their complete-report/decisive-value verifier, 5/5. This extends
cross-host evidence without changing the original nonportable adaptive row.
`make verify-canonical-adaptive PYTHON=python3 EVIDENCE_DIR=/path/to/new-evidence`
selects the input audit and canonical probe explicitly; default geometry/full
verification retains the original fixture and its known cross-host failure.

Evidence: `reports/adaptive_canonical{,_modal}.json`, the complete canonical
capture, `reports/canonical_geometry_cross_host.json`,
`reports/recipe_verify_modal.json`, and `reports/canonical_verify_selected.json`.
The selected public verifier passes 5/5 and retains all 23,854,680 capture bytes.
Each distinct geometry command uses a fresh tree and pinned reference resources;
only explicitly shared chain/loft execution reuses results within that invocation.

The complete six-row geometry group passes locally with command isolation.
The selected audit/canonical public verifier also passes on Modal in 19.12 s:
its complete verification JSON and all 23,854,680 captured bytes match the local
verifier exactly. Twenty-nine controls include an independent two-command
falsifier: the first command changes its resource copy from 0 to 19, while the
second still observes the original 0. Selected execution reports the omitted
first command explicitly, and the original resource remains unchanged.
Receipts: `reports/geometry_verify_isolated.json`,
`reports/canonical_verify_modal.json`, and `reports/verify_controls.json`.
Current coverage is 24/164 fresh rows mapped, 140 unmapped and three GPU rows
deferred. The default strict geometry verifier retains OWN-GATE-FAIL for its
original cross-host fixture; the explicit canonical selection is the passing
portable scope. No full-repository green result is claimed.

### Additional recipe selftests preregistration

Run the unchanged executor (15 top-level case gates plus its aggregate first
case gate and nested draft falsifier), recipe cache (seven case gates), feature
builder (13 identified checks) and B-rep recipe synthesizer (five uppercase case
gates) twice in independent CPU trees. Require their original overall gates,
all specified cases, and exact complete canonical reports. For the cache only,
exclude these six predeclared observational timing leaves from report identity:
`AT5c_synthetic_full_hit.{t_cold_s,t_warm_s,speedup_x}` and
`AT6a_mem_hit_same_process.{t_cold_s,t_mem_s,speedup_x}`. Retain the complete raw
reports and timing values. These times are not acceptance gates in the frozen
cache selftest; all cache hit, mismatch, memory ownership and volume gates remain
mandatory. Any other report difference remains a failed reproduction.

The initial extended capture passes every own gate in both runs. Cache and
feature reports match under the preregistered comparison. Executor full-report
identity fails solely at `AT2b_tomcut_fallbevis.row.wall_ms`; the synthesizer
fails solely at five generated `step` paths. Preserve these strict-report
negatives. A separately declared metadata projection will omit that one measured
time and retain only the filename for the five temporary STEP locations. All
other fields, filenames, own gates and numerical values must still match. Raw
reports and every observational value remain retained; no geometric tolerance or
cache acceptance rule changes. Projection controls must reject attempts to omit
gate/geometry fields and must detect changed numerical values after projection.

### Extended recipe verification result

All eight executable recipe selftests pass the public verifier locally, 5/5:
69 identified case gates plus the executor's combined first-case and nested draft
falsifier checks. All declared report fields and decisive numbers match; only
specified observational clocks and generated STEP directory names are projected.
The initial strict-report failures remain `reports/recipe_extended_initial.json`,
with all eight complete initial raw reports under `reports/recipe_extended_initial/`.
`reports/recipe_extended_projection.json` records exact agreement under the
separate declared projection. Fresh verification is
`reports/recipe_verify_extended.json`; every raw fresh report is also retained in
the caller evidence directory.

Decisive values include 21 declared operations / 20 handlers, all 17 cache hits,
13 feature checks with zero failures, and the unchanged asymmetric-wedge excesses
360.0000000001746 / 360.0000000000291 mm3. Thirty-eight controls explicitly reject
omitting geometry or gate fields, require STEP filenames to remain distinguishable,
detect changed geometry/gates after projection and distinguish boolean true from
integer one. Current coverage is 28/164 fresh rows, 136 unmapped and three GPU
rows deferred; full coverage and original unordered cross-host failure remain open.

The unchanged eight-selftest public recipe verifier passes all five aggregate

`reports/recipe_extended_cross_host.json`.

### Form-verb artifact result

All eleven case gates, eight nested falsifiers, both pattern solid-count gates,

### Sketch artifact verification result

All eleven original value assertions and four additional predicates pass in two

preregistered clocks and twelve validated self-reference locations are projected.

repetition counts remain exact; raw reports and all clock observations are retained.

The public eleven-recipe route passes 5/5 locally with 95 identified case/value
gates plus additional executor, form-verb and sketch predicates. Controls reject
an omitted analytic assertion, changed coordinate, zero/non-finite timing median,
wrong dof, missing conflict explanation, an invalid geometry marked green,

timing. Original and candidate libraries are also compared within one process
to distinguish the code change from cross-run scheduling variation.

processes. Minimum Python/candidate ratios are 1.272186x locally and

the same full-field and 1.25x requirements; its minimum is 1.255965x. The
original C/candidate ratio is only 1.004381–1.025103x over those eight
observations. This is a small reader improvement, not a broad pipeline speedup.

### Native two-hit compaction candidate

A complete original-stage observation measures 1.561–1.719 ms sorting and
compacting 27,141 L columns (42,242 hits), within 8.745 / 9.907 ms full
two-hit evaluations. The separate native compactor replaces NumPy boolean
gathers and two-hit swap arrays by one bounded ordered loop. The C++ loop
compares the same depths and signs and copies the original double bits;
equal keys keep input order. Wider rows and other dtypes retain the original
sorter. No RT, mask, EDT, mesh or arithmetic tolerance changes.

The existing `field_two_hit_stage_probe.py` accepts `--sort-library` and
`--output-prefix` to exercise this separate seam under its unchanged five
gates: 14 full CPU-exact fields, exact repeats, 17 controls, and both L
full evaluations >=1.25x faster than general row sorting. Complete changed
fields and captured actual raw hit rows will be retained, including failures.

The native-compaction variant passes all 5/5 original stage gates in two
independent L4 processes. Plate-L full stage takes 6.325–6.431 ms, versus
9.341–9.675 ms for general row sorting in the same process and reversed
orders: 1.463313–1.504532x, above the unchanged 1.25x gate. The entire
70-array capture (7,192,056 bytes per process) matches the frozen original
two-hit capture exactly. All 17 original controls pass in each process.
This is a direct-stage result; it does not satisfy the separate 2 ms service
gate, and it does not change RT or EDT kernel execution.

Local CPU execution of all captured raw L4 hit rows matches the complete
stored sorted columns/depths/signs and the original NumPy sorter in both
orders. The large row set takes 0.240–0.245 ms versus 0.974–1.075 ms for
NumPy locally. Nine CPU controls pass, including two invalid native counts.
An independent ASan/UBSan executable passes 11 cases, including equal-key
stability, signed-zero bits, empty rows, the maximum row bound and refusal
before any output write for invalid counts.

```sh
c++ -std=c++17 -O3 -fno-fast-math -ffp-contract=off -shared -fPIC \
  src/field_engine/rt_hit_compact_v1/compact.cpp -o /tmp/field_hit_compact.so
# With the same RT, mask, paired EDT and fixture environment as the original probe:
python probes/field_engine/field_two_hit_stage_probe.py \
  --sort-library /tmp/field_hit_compact.so --output-prefix /tmp/field_compact_hits_new
c++ -std=c++17 -O1 -g -fno-fast-math -ffp-contract=off \
  -fno-omit-frame-pointer -fsanitize=address,undefined -fno-sanitize-recover=all \
  tests/native_hit_compact_sanitizer.cpp src/field_engine/rt_hit_compact_v1/compact.cpp \
  -o /tmp/field_hit_compact_sanitizer
/tmp/field_hit_compact_sanitizer
```

## Diagnostic probes

These programs retain their own gates and original failed attempts. They are
not additional engine implementations. Run them directly from their new paths;
the bootstrap adds only repository-local import paths. Native probe libraries
retain their engine includes at their original source locations.
